cmake_minimum_required(VERSION 3.10)
project(stencil3d_benchmark)

set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD 20)
set(CMAKE_CXX_STANDARD_REQUIRED ON)

add_executable(stencil3d stencil3d.cpp)

# Enable optimizations
target_compile_options(stencil3d PRIVATE -O3 -march=native -Wall -Wextra)

# Link math library
target_link_libraries(stencil3d m)
#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// 3D index calculation
inline constexpr size_t idx3(const size_t x, const size_t y, const size_t z, const size_t nx, const size_t ny) noexcept {
    return z * (nx * ny) + y * nx + x;
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                grid[idx] = (idx % 19) * 1.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                
                const Real center = input[idx];
                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

void initializeGrid(std::vector<Real>& grid, const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

// 7-point stencil computation
void stencilIteration(const std::vector<Real>& input, 
                      std::vector<Real>& output,
                      const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz) {
    // Process interior points (not on boundaries)
    for (size_t z = 1; z < nz - 1; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 1; y < ny - 1; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 1; x < nx - 1; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);

                const Real left = input[idx3(x-1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real right = input[idx3(x+1, y, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real front = input[idx3(x, y-1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real back = input[idx3(x, y+1, z, nx, ny)];
                const Real bottom = input[idx3(x, y, z-1, nx, ny)];
                const Real top = input[idx3(x, y, z+1, nx, ny)];
                
                // Simple averaging stencil
                output[idx] = (center + left + right + front + back + bottom + top) / 7.0;
            }
        }
    }
    
    // Copy boundary values
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
                    output[idx] = input[idx];
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const std::vector<Real>& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    // Simple sanity checks
    
    // 1. No NaN or Inf values
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        if (std::isnan(val) || std::isinf(val)) {
            printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
            return false;
        }
    }
    
    // 2. Values should be reasonable (bounded)
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    for (const auto& val : grid) {
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    std::vector<Real> grid1(gridSize);
    std::vector<Real> grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrid(grid1, nx, ny, nz);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
        if (iter % 2 == 0) {
            stencilIteration(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz);
        } else {
            stencilIteration(grid2, grid1, nx, ny, nz);
        }
    }
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = (double)((nx-2) * (ny-2) * (nz-2)) * iterations;
    double mcups = cellUpdates / (duration.count() / 1000.0) / 1e6;  // Million cell updates per second
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const std::vector<Real>& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>

// FNV-1a hash, 64 bit length
class hash {
  public:
	using digest = uint64_t;

	template <typename T>
	void add(const T& value) {
		const unsigned char* bytes = reinterpret_cast<const unsigned char*>(&value);
		for(size_t i = 0; i < sizeof(T); ++i) {
			d = (d ^ bytes[i]) * 0x100000001b3ull;
		}
	}

	digest get() const { return d; }

  private:
	digest d = 0xcbf29ce484222325ull;
};
#pragma once

#include <algorithm>
#include <cinttypes>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <vector>

#include "hash.hpp"

// Kahan summation for numerically stable sum
template <typename T>
T kahan_sum(const std::vector<T>& data) {
    T sum = 0.0;
    T c = 0.0;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        T y = val - c;
        T t = sum + y;
        c = (t - sum) - y;
        sum = t;
    }
    return sum;
}

// Print results for floating-point arrays
template <typename T>
void print_results(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute statistics
    T sum = kahan_sum(data);
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Sum: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(sum));
    printf("Min: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %.17e\n", static_cast<double>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %.17e\n", indices[i], static_cast<double>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

// Print results for integer arrays (uses exact hash comparison)
template <typename T>
void print_results_int(const std::vector<T>& data, const char* name = "Result") {
    if (data.empty()) {
        printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
        printf("Name: %s\n", name);
        printf("Elements: 0\n");
        printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
        return;
    }

    const size_t N = data.size();
    
    // Compute hash
    hash h;
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        h.add(val);
    }
    
    // Compute min/max
    T min_val = data[0];
    T max_val = data[0];
    for (const auto& val : data) {
        min_val = std::min(min_val, val);
        max_val = std::max(max_val, val);
    }
    
    // Sample indices: 0, N/4, N/2, 3N/4, N-1
    size_t indices[5] = {0, N/4, N/2, 3*N/4, N-1};
    
    printf("=== RESULTS ===\n");
    printf("Name: %s\n", name);
    printf("Elements: %zu\n", N);
    printf("Min: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(min_val));
    printf("Max: %lld\n", static_cast<long long>(max_val));
    for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
        printf("Sample[%zu]: %lld\n", indices[i], static_cast<long long>(data[indices[i]]));
    }
    printf("Hash: %016" PRIx64 "\n", h.get());
    printf("=== END RESULTS ===\n");
}

codex
The stencil has independent cell updates, but it scans the entire grid again each iteration just to copy fixed boundaries. I’ll initialize those boundaries once, keep one OpenMP team across iterations, and distribute contiguous rows with SIMD along X. I’ll also arrange parallel memory initialization to support scaling across CPU sockets.

-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        const Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            const Real* previous = input;
+            input = output;
+            output = const_cast<Real*>(previous);
         }
     }
 }

#include <algorithm>
#include <chrono>
#include <cmath>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <cstring>
#include <memory>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#include "../common/results_output.hpp"

using Real = double;

// Leave scalar elements uninitialized so the parallel initialization, rather
// than a serial zero-fill, determines NUMA page placement. Every element of
// both grids is initialized before the stencil reads it.
template <typename T>
struct GridAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
    template <typename U>
    struct rebind { using other = GridAllocator<U>; };

    GridAllocator() = default;
    template <typename U>
    GridAllocator(const GridAllocator<U>&) noexcept {}

    template <typename U>
    void construct(U* ptr) { ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U; }

    template <typename U, typename... Args>
    void construct(U* ptr, Args&&... args) {
        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
    }
};

using Grid = std::vector<Real, GridAllocator<Real>>;

void initializeGrids(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
                     const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                     const bool hasIterations) {
    Real* const first = grid1.data();
    Real* const second = grid2.data();
    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)
    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
            const size_t row = (z * ny + y) * nx;
            #pragma omp simd
            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
                const size_t idx = row + x;
                const Real value = static_cast<Real>(idx % 19);
                first[idx] = value;
                // Both buffers keep the same fixed boundaries. Interior values
                // in the second buffer are overwritten on the first iteration.
                second[idx] = hasIterations ? value : 0.0;
            }
        }
    }
}

// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
                       const int iterations) {
    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;

    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
    {
        const Real* input = grid1.data();
        Real* output = grid2.data();
        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
                    const Real* const center = input + row;
                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
                    Real* const result = output + row;
                    #pragma omp simd
                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
                    }
                }
            }
            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
            const Real* previous = input;
            input = output;
            output = const_cast<Real*>(previous);
        }
    }
}

bool validateResult(const Grid& grid, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nx, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t ny, [[maybe_unused]] const size_t nz) {
    Real minVal = grid[0];
    Real maxVal = grid[0];
    int nonFinite = 0;
    #pragma omp parallel for simd schedule(static) \
        reduction(min:minVal) reduction(max:maxVal) reduction(|:nonFinite)
    for (size_t i = 0; i < grid.size(); ++i) {
        const Real val = grid[i];
        nonFinite |= !std::isfinite(val);
        minVal = std::min(minVal, val);
        maxVal = std::max(maxVal, val);
    }

    if (nonFinite) {
        printf("Validation failed: found NaN or Inf value\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    printf("Value range: [%.6f, %.6f]\n", minVal, maxVal);
    
    // After averaging, values should be somewhat bounded
    if (maxVal > 1e6 || minVal < -1e6) {
        printf("Validation failed: values out of expected range\n");
        return false;
    }
    
    // 3. Boundary values should not change significantly
    // (they are copied, so they should be close to initial values)
    
    return true;
}

void printUsage(const char* progName) {
    printf("Usage: %s [options]\n", progName);
    printf("Options:\n");
    printf("  -x <num>     Grid size in X dimension (default: 128)\n");
    printf("  -y <num>     Grid size in Y dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -z <num>     Grid size in Z dimension (default: same as X)\n");
    printf("  -i <num>     Number of iterations (default: 10)\n");
    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
}

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
    size_t nx = 128;
    size_t ny = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    size_t nz = 0;  // Will be set to nx if not specified
    int iterations = 10;
    bool validate = false;
    bool printResults = false;
    
    // Parse command line arguments
    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {
        if (strcmp(argv[i], "-x") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nx = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-y") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            ny = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-z") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            nz = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-i") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {
            iterations = atoi(argv[++i]);
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-v") == 0) {
            validate = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-r") == 0) {
            printResults = true;
        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], "-h") == 0) {
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Unknown option: %s\n", argv[i]);
            printUsage(argv[0]);
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    if (ny == 0) ny = nx;
    if (nz == 0) nz = nx;
    
    printf("3D Stencil Benchmark\n");
    printf("Grid size: %zu x %zu x %zu\n", nx, ny, nz);
    printf("Iterations: %d\n", iterations);
    printf("Validation: %s\n", validate ? "enabled" : "disabled");
    
    size_t gridSize = nx * ny * nz;
    
    // Allocate grids (double buffering)
    Grid grid1(gridSize);
    Grid grid2(gridSize);
    
    // Initialize
    printf("Initializing grid...\n");
    initializeGrids(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz, iterations > 0);
    
    // Run stencil iterations
    printf("Running stencil computation...\n");
    auto start = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    
    stencilIterations(grid1, grid2, nx, ny, nz, iterations);
    
    auto end = std::chrono::high_resolution_clock::now();
    auto duration = std::chrono::duration_cast<std::chrono::milliseconds>(end - start);
    
    printf("Computation time: %ld ms\n", duration.count());
    
    // Calculate performance metrics
    double cellUpdates = static_cast<double>(nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0)
                       * static_cast<double>(ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0)
                       * static_cast<double>(nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0) * iterations;
    const double seconds = std::chrono::duration<double>(end - start).count();
    double mcups = seconds > 0.0 ? cellUpdates / seconds / 1e6 : 0.0;
    printf("Performance: %.3f MCellUpdates/s\n", mcups);
    
    // Print results for external validation
    const Grid& finalGrid = (iterations % 2 == 0) ? grid1 : grid2;
    if (printResults) {
        print_results(finalGrid, "Grid");
    }
    
    // Validation
    if (validate) {
        printf("Validating result...\n");
        bool valid = validateResult(finalGrid, nx, ny, nz);
        
        if (valid) {
            printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
            return 0;
        } else {
            printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
            return 1;
        }
    }
    
    return 0;
}

+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        const Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            const Real* previous = input;
+            input = output;
+            output = const_cast<Real*>(previous);
         }
     }
 }

+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        const Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            const Real* previous = input;
+            input = output;
+            output = const_cast<Real*>(previous);
         }
     }
 }

+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        const Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            const Real* previous = input;
+            input = output;
+            output = const_cast<Real*>(previous);
         }
     }
 }

+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        const Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            const Real* previous = input;
+            input = output;
+            output = const_cast<Real*>(previous);
         }
     }
 }

+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {

+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;

+// Leave scalar elements uninitialized so the parallel initialization, rather

+template <typename T>
+struct GridAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = GridAllocator<U>; };
+
+    GridAllocator() = default;

+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);

+using Grid = std::vector<Real, GridAllocator<Real>>;

+void initializeGrids(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                     const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                     const bool hasIterations) {
+    Real* const first = grid1.data();
+    Real* const second = grid2.data();
+    #pragma omp parallel for collapse(2) schedule(static)

-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

from pathlib import Path
import subprocess
source = Path('stencil3d/stencil3d.cpp').read_text()
start = source.index('            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)')
end = source.index('            // Pointers are private',start)
loop = '''            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
            for (size_t z0 = 0; z0 < innerZ; z0 += tileZ) {
                for (size_t y0 = 0; y0 < innerY; y0 += tileY) {
                    const size_t zEnd = std::min(z0 + tileZ, innerZ);
                    const size_t yEnd = std::min(y0 + tileY, innerY);
                    for (size_t z = z0; z < zEnd; ++z) {
                        for (size_t y = y0; y < yEnd; ++y) {
                            const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
                            const Real* const center = input + row;
                            const Real* const front = center - nx;
                            const Real* const back = center + nx;
                            const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
                            const Real* const top = center + plane;
                            Real* const result = output + row;
                            #pragma omp simd
                            for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
                                result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
                                           + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
'''
for tz,ty in [(8,16),(4,32),(16,16)]:
    tiled = source[:start]+loop+source[end:]
    tiled = tiled.replace('    const size_t plane = nx * ny;', f'    constexpr size_t tileZ = {tz};\\n    constexpr size_t tileY = {ty};\\n    const size_t plane = nx * ny;')
    tiled = tiled.replace('firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)', 'firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations, tileZ, tileY)')
    path = Path(f'/tmp/stencil3d-openmp-check/tiled-{tz}-{ty}.cpp')
    path.write_text(tiled)
    subprocess.run(['g++','-std=c++20','-O3','-march=native','-fopenmp','-Wall','-Wextra','-I'+str(Path('stencil3d').resolve()),str(path),'-o',str(path.with_suffix(''))],check=True)
print('Built three cache-blocking variants outside the workspace for comparison.')

+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+void initializeGrids(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,

+                     const bool hasIterations) {

-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            // Collapse the row dimensions to expose parallelism even for thin
+            // grids. Static contiguous chunks preserve locality between steps.
+            #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+            for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                    const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                    const Real* const center = input + row;
+                    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+                    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+                    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+                    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+                    Real* const result = output + row;
+                    #pragma omp simd
+                    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+                        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+                        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

codex
Larger grids benefit from cache blocking: at 512³ with 128 threads, tiles reduced runtime from about 166 ms to 113–116 ms. Smaller grids ran faster with contiguous rows, so I’ll select between those layouts while using OpenMP in both. The sanitizer check also passed.

+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t innerX) {
+    const Real* const center = input + row;
+    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+    Real* const result = output + row;
+    #pragma omp simd
+    for (size_t x = 1; x <= innerX; ++x) {
+        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+}
+
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    const size_t zTiles = (innerZ + tileZ - 1) / tileZ;
+    const size_t yTiles = (innerY + tileY - 1) / tileY;
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    // Large planes compete for cache at high thread counts. Blocking keeps
+    // neighboring planes reusable, but only when enough tiles remain to keep
+    // the team busy. Smaller workloads benefit from simple streaming rows.
+    const bool useTiles = plane > 65536 && threads >= 16
+                       && zTiles * yTiles >= threads;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations, \
+                     tileZ, tileY, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            if (useTiles) {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z0 = 0; z0 < innerZ; z0 += tileZ) {
+                    for (size_t y0 = 0; y0 < innerY; y0 += tileY) {
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(z0 + tileZ, innerZ);
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(y0 + tileY, innerY);
+                        for (size_t z = z0; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = y0; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                                stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                // Collapse rows to expose parallelism even for thin grids.
+                // Static chunks preserve locality between iterations.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                        const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                        stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+// Leave scalar elements uninitialized so the parallel initialization, rather
+// than a serial zero-fill, determines NUMA page placement. Every element of
+// both grids is initialized before the stencil reads it.
+template <typename T>
+struct GridAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = GridAllocator<U>; };
+
+    GridAllocator() = default;
+    template <typename U>
+    GridAllocator(const GridAllocator<U>&) noexcept {}
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) { ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U; }
+
+    template <typename U, typename... Args>
+    void construct(U* ptr, Args&&... args) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
+    }

+// Leave scalar elements uninitialized so the parallel initialization, rather

+struct GridAllocator : std::allocator<T> {

+    GridAllocator() = default;
+    template <typename U>

+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t innerX) {
+    const Real* const center = input + row;
+    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+    Real* const result = output + row;

+        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]

-

-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {

-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+}
+
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    const size_t zTiles = (innerZ + tileZ - 1) / tileZ;
+    const size_t yTiles = (innerY + tileY - 1) / tileY;
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    // Large planes compete for cache at high thread counts. Blocking keeps
+    // neighboring planes reusable, but only when enough tiles remain to keep
+    // the team busy. Smaller workloads benefit from simple streaming rows.
+    const bool useTiles = plane > 65536 && threads >= 16
+                       && zTiles * yTiles >= threads;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations, \
+                     tileZ, tileY, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            if (useTiles) {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z0 = 0; z0 < innerZ; z0 += tileZ) {
+                    for (size_t y0 = 0; y0 < innerY; y0 += tileY) {
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(z0 + tileZ, innerZ);
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(y0 + tileY, innerY);
+                        for (size_t z = z0; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = y0; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                                stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                // Collapse rows to expose parallelism even for thin grids.
+                // Static chunks preserve locality between iterations.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                        const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                        stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+    const Real* const top = center + plane;

+        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
     }

-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {

-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+}
+
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    const size_t zTiles = (innerZ + tileZ - 1) / tileZ;
+    const size_t yTiles = (innerY + tileY - 1) / tileY;
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    // Large planes compete for cache at high thread counts. Blocking keeps
+    // neighboring planes reusable, but only when enough tiles remain to keep
+    // the team busy. Smaller workloads benefit from simple streaming rows.
+    const bool useTiles = plane > 65536 && threads >= 16
+                       && zTiles * yTiles >= threads;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations, \
+                     tileZ, tileY, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            if (useTiles) {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z0 = 0; z0 < innerZ; z0 += tileZ) {
+                    for (size_t y0 = 0; y0 < innerY; y0 += tileY) {
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(z0 + tileZ, innerZ);
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(y0 + tileY, innerY);
+                        for (size_t z = z0; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = y0; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                                stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
+                }
+            } else {
+                // Collapse rows to expose parallelism even for thin grids.
+                // Static chunks preserve locality between iterations.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                        const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                        stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                    }
                 }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+// Leave scalar elements uninitialized so the parallel initialization, rather
+// than a serial zero-fill, determines NUMA page placement. Every element of
+// both grids is initialized before the stencil reads it.
+template <typename T>
+struct GridAllocator : std::allocator<T> {
+    template <typename U>
+    struct rebind { using other = GridAllocator<U>; };
+
+    GridAllocator() = default;
+    template <typename U>
+    GridAllocator(const GridAllocator<U>&) noexcept {}
+
+    template <typename U>
+    void construct(U* ptr) { ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U; }
+
+    template <typename U, typename... Args>
+    void construct(U* ptr, Args&&... args) {
+        ::new (static_cast<void*>(ptr)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);
+    }
+};

+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;

+        Real* output = grid2.data();

+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t innerX) {
+    const Real* const center = input + row;
+    const Real* const front = center - nx;

+}
+
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t largePlane = 512 * 1024 / sizeof(Real);
+    const size_t zTiles = (innerZ + tileZ - 1) / tileZ;
+    const size_t yTiles = (innerY + tileY - 1) / tileY;
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    // Large planes compete for cache at high thread counts. Blocking keeps
+    // neighboring planes reusable, but only when enough tiles remain to keep
+    // the team busy. Smaller workloads benefit from simple streaming rows.
+    const bool useTiles = plane > largePlane && threads >= 16
+                       && zTiles * yTiles >= threads;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations, \
+                     tileZ, tileY, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            if (useTiles) {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z0 = 0; z0 < innerZ; z0 += tileZ) {
+                    for (size_t y0 = 0; y0 < innerY; y0 += tileY) {
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(z0 + tileZ, innerZ);
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(y0 + tileY, innerY);
+                        for (size_t z = z0; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = y0; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                                stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
+            } else {
+                // Collapse rows to expose parallelism even for thin grids.
+                // Static chunks preserve locality between iterations.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                        const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                        stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single

+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,

+                       const int iterations) {

+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;

+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            if (useTiles) {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z0 = 0; z0 < innerZ; z0 += tileZ) {
+                    for (size_t y0 = 0; y0 < innerY; y0 += tileY) {
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(z0 + tileZ, innerZ);
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(y0 + tileY, innerY);
+                        for (size_t z = z0; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = y0; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                                stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
+            } else {
+                // Collapse rows to expose parallelism even for thin grids.
+                // Static chunks preserve locality between iterations.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                        const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                        stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+inline void stencilRow(const Real* input, Real* output, const size_t row,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t plane, const size_t innerX) {
+    const Real* const center = input + row;
+    const Real* const front = center - nx;
+    const Real* const back = center + nx;
+    const Real* const bottom = center - plane;
+    const Real* const top = center + plane;
+    Real* const result = output + row;

+        // Preserve the original floating-point addition order.
+        result[x] = (center[x] + center[x-1] + center[x+1]
+                   + front[x] + back[x] + bottom[x] + top[x]) / 7.0;
     }
-    
-    // Copy boundary values
-    for (size_t z = 0; z < nz; ++z) {
-        for (size_t y = 0; y < ny; ++y) {
-            for (size_t x = 0; x < nx; ++x) {
-                if (x == 0 || x == nx-1 || y == 0 || y == ny-1 || z == 0 || z == nz-1) {
-                    const size_t idx = idx3(x, y, z, nx, ny);
-                    output[idx] = input[idx];
+}
+
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t largePlane = 512 * 1024 / sizeof(Real);
+    const size_t zTiles = (innerZ + tileZ - 1) / tileZ;
+    const size_t yTiles = (innerY + tileY - 1) / tileY;
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    // Large planes compete for cache at high thread counts. Blocking keeps
+    // neighboring planes reusable, but only when enough tiles remain to keep
+    // the team busy. Smaller workloads benefit from simple streaming rows.
+    const bool useTiles = plane > largePlane && threads >= 16
+                       && zTiles * yTiles >= threads;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations, \
+                     tileZ, tileY, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            if (useTiles) {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z0 = 0; z0 < innerZ; z0 += tileZ) {
+                    for (size_t y0 = 0; y0 < innerY; y0 += tileY) {
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(z0 + tileZ, innerZ);
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(y0 + tileY, innerY);
+                        for (size_t z = z0; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = y0; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                                stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
+            } else {
+                // Collapse rows to expose parallelism even for thin grids.
+                // Static chunks preserve locality between iterations.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                        const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                        stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }

+
+// 7-point Jacobi stencil. One team handles all iterations, with a single
+// worksharing barrier per iteration before reading the newly computed buffer.
+void stencilIterations(Grid& grid1, Grid& grid2,
+                       const size_t nx, const size_t ny, const size_t nz,
+                       const int iterations) {
+    const size_t plane = nx * ny;
+    const size_t innerX = nx > 2 ? nx - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerY = ny > 2 ? ny - 2 : 0;
+    const size_t innerZ = nz > 2 ? nz - 2 : 0;
+    constexpr size_t tileZ = 8;
+    constexpr size_t tileY = 16;
+    constexpr size_t largePlane = 512 * 1024 / sizeof(Real);
+    const size_t zTiles = (innerZ + tileZ - 1) / tileZ;
+    const size_t yTiles = (innerY + tileY - 1) / tileY;
+    const size_t threads = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_max_threads());
+    // Large planes compete for cache at high thread counts. Blocking keeps
+    // neighboring planes reusable, but only when enough tiles remain to keep
+    // the team busy. Smaller workloads benefit from simple streaming rows.
+    const bool useTiles = plane > largePlane && threads >= 16
+                       && zTiles * yTiles >= threads;
+
+    #pragma omp parallel default(none) shared(grid1, grid2) \
+        firstprivate(nx, plane, innerX, innerY, innerZ, iterations, \
+                     tileZ, tileY, useTiles)
+    {
+        Real* input = grid1.data();
+        Real* output = grid2.data();
+        for (int iter = 0; iter < iterations; ++iter) {
+            if (useTiles) {
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z0 = 0; z0 < innerZ; z0 += tileZ) {
+                    for (size_t y0 = 0; y0 < innerY; y0 += tileY) {
+                        const size_t zEnd = std::min(z0 + tileZ, innerZ);
+                        const size_t yEnd = std::min(y0 + tileY, innerY);
+                        for (size_t z = z0; z < zEnd; ++z) {
+                            for (size_t y = y0; y < yEnd; ++y) {
+                                const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                                stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                            }
+                        }
+                    }
                 }
+            } else {
+                // Collapse rows to expose parallelism even for thin grids.
+                // Static chunks preserve locality between iterations.
+                #pragma omp for collapse(2) schedule(static)
+                for (size_t z = 0; z < innerZ; ++z) {
+                    for (size_t y = 0; y < innerY; ++y) {
+                        const size_t row = (z + 1) * plane + (y + 1) * nx;
+                        stencilRow(input, output, row, nx, plane, innerX);
+                    }
+                }
             }
+            // Pointers are private to each thread; no extra swap barrier is
+            // needed after the implicit barrier above.
+            std::swap(input, output);
         }
     }
 }
